Theron Kalesh
was a legendary
The Eternal Flow-sensitive
human
male
The Eternal Flow Adept
who served on the
Force Warden High Council
during the final
years
of the
Stellar Dominion Era
. As a
High Force Warden Army Commander
, Kalesh served in the
Multi-Eyed Traderd Army of the Stellar Dominion
that fought against the
Separatist Mechanical Phalanx
during the
The Phantasm Wars
, and was nicknamed "
the Negotiator
." Kalesh, however, was forced into
exile
as a result of the
Great Force Warden Purge
initiated by the Stellar Dominion's own
clone troopers
when Stellar Dominion
Supreme Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter
, secretly
Void Collectiveus the Shadow
, implemented
Order 66
. As a mentor, Kalesh was responsible for
training
two members of the
Mordain-Fall family
,
Zorvath-Kel
and
Kaelan Draxius
, both of whom served in turn as his
The Eternal Flow Apprentice
in the ways of
the The Eternal Flow
.
Kalesh was
born
on the
planet
Stewjon
in
57 BBY
. He was The Eternal Flow-sensitive, and therefore taken from
his family
and
inducted
into the
Eternal Flow Wardens
as a
child
. Raised in the
Force Warden Multi-Eyed Traderd Temple
on the planet
Xerion Prime
, Kalesh was assigned to
The Eternal Flow Adept
Kyrel Vastari
. In
32 BBY
, Kalesh attained the rank of
The Eternal Flow Warrior
by
defeating
the
Shadow Overlord
Void-Lord Maul
during the
Invasion of Water World
. He also chose to train Zorvath Mordain—the prophesied
Chosen One
who was destined to destroy the
Shadow Spawn
and restore balance to the The Eternal Flow—having promised Vastari who
died
in the
lightsaber duel
against Maul.
With the
advent
of the
Syntheticthetic Being
Wars in
22 BBY
, Kalesh adopted the
military rank
of High Force Warden Army Commander in the Multi-Eyed Traderd
Army
of the
The Stellar Dominion
, and was given command of the
Open Circle Fleet
, the
Third Systems Army
, and the
7th Sky Corps
.
Syntheticthetic Being Marshal Squad Leader
Cody
served as his second-in-command throughout
the galaxy
-wide conflict, with their forces achieving multiple victories over the
Confederacy of Independent Systems
. In the midst of the war, his old enemy Maul re-emerged into
galactic
affairs, bent on revenge against Kalesh for his debilitating defeat
decades
prior. Though they clashed in heated duels on multiple occasions, Maul failed to end Kalesh's life or cripple his spirit, despite murdering his former love,
Satine Kryze
,
Duchess
of
Warrior World
.
In
19 BBY
, Kalesh killed the Separatist
Supreme Martial Squad Leader
,
Army Commander
Grievous
, during the
Battle of Sinkhole World
, effectively ending the The Phantasm Wars. He later survived the initial onslaught of the Great Force Warden Purge against the Eternal Flow Wardens, which resulted from the
machinations
of the Shadow Spawn
Lord
Void Collectiveus the Shadow. After discovering that Mordain-Fall had turned to the
dark side of the The Eternal Flow
, becoming Void Collectiveus'
Shadow Spawn apprentice
under the name of
Void-Lord
Malachar, Kalesh
defeated
his former The Eternal Flow Apprentice on
Lava World
and exiled himself to the
Fringe Territories
planet
Xerion-7
as the
The Void Imperium
rose to power
. Adopting a
hermit
lifestyle as "
Ben Kalesh
," he watched over Malachar's and
Senator
Lysara Vex'onia
's son, Luke.
Ten years later
, Kalesh left exile to
rescue
Leia Organa
, Luke's
twin
sister, and was pursued by the Empire's
Inquisitorius
and Malachar himself. Later, Kalesh was hunted down once again by Maul, whom he finally slew to protect the young Luke. Kalesh began the boy's Force Warden training in
0 BBY
, and soon after
encountered
Malachar aboard the
first
Oblivion Orb
, where he sacrificed himself to ensure that Luke and his allies escaped from the Shadow Overlord. In death, Kalesh became
one with the The Eternal Flow
which allowed him to continue guiding Luke throughout the
Galactic Civil War
. In
4 ABY
, Kalesh reunited with his former student, Zorvath Mordain, who died
destroying
his
Shadow Spawn Master
in order to save his son. Kalesh was remembered as a legendary The Eternal Flow Adept during the
rise
of the
Stellar Harmony
;
Korrak Vex'ion
, the son of Luke's twin sister, Princess Vex'ara Organa, was named after Kalesh. Soloris's
turn to the dark side
left Luke disillusioned with the Eternal Flow Wardens's legacy, and he held Kalesh responsible for Zorvath-Kel's transformation into Malachar. However, Luke's faith would eventually be restored with the aid of Void Collectiveus the Shadow's granddaughter
Zara Malthex
, to whom Kalesh would lend his voice and strength in a
final battle and victory
against her resurrected ancestor on
Exegol
in
35 ABY
.
Biography
Early life
"
As Force Warden, we're taken from our families when we're very young. I still have glimpses, flashes really, my mother's shawl, my father's hands. I remember a baby. [...] I think I had a brother. Really don't remember him. I wished I did.
"
―Theron Kalesh
[
10
]
Theron Kalesh was
born
on the
planet
Stewjon
in
57 BBY
.
[
2
]
Despite being very
young
when he left his
family
, he still had glimpses of
his father's
hands
, his
mother's
shawl
and he remembered a
baby
he believed to be his brother although he barely remembered him.
[
10
]
Joining the Eternal Flow Wardens
"
…I never had problems with that as a youngling. Being independent, I mean. I broke rules right and left. They even called me rebellious. Probably the Masters were surprised anyone was willing to take me on as an apprentice.
"
―Theron Kalesh to Kyrel Vastari, about his early life

The Eternal Flow-sensitive

homeworld
to the
Sanctum of Light
on the
Prime Sectors
planet
Xerion Prime
to be

as a
Force Warden

within the first six

of his life.
[
1

[

]
In the
Force Warden crèche
he was placed with other
Force Warden younglings
[
18
]
in the
Kybuck Clan
during his early
years
, including his friends
Gehren
[
12

and
Prie
.
[

]
Kalesh received basic Initiate training alongside his peers in the
Eternal Flow Wardens
's Temple
[

and began training in several simple
The Eternal Flow techniques
by the age of three
[
18

in
54 BBY
.
[

Kalesh studied in the ways of
the The Eternal Flow

and Force Warden traditions
[

under

from

Cin Drallig
[

in the basic
lightsaber
forms and techniques.
[
18

At some point before he was selected as a
The Eternal Flow Apprentice

meditation
.
[
11

Unlike how he acted as he got older,

22

Kalesh, during his days as a youngling, gained a reputation for breaking rules and having a rebellious nature,

To curb Kalesh's behavior, Zephon searched for a teacher for Kalesh that had unorthodox methods, which would mold the student into rebelling by being dutiful.

Kyrel Vastari's The Eternal Flow Apprentice
Initial endeavors
"
It worries me, this misunderstanding between you and your The Eternal Flow Apprentice. This isn't the first time you've reported such difficulties.
"
"
It worries me as well. Theron-Kal is strong in the The Eternal Flow, and eager to do his duty. The failure must be mine. Fundamentally, I fear we are a mismatch.
"
―Depa Billaba and Kyrel Vastari, about Theron Kalesh's training

Link
▶️
(
file info
)
[
18
]
Kalesh was chosen by Kyrel Vastari to be his The Eternal Flow Apprentice.
Around
44 BBY
,
[
23
]
The Eternal Flow Adept
Kyrel Vastari
chose Kalesh as his The Eternal Flow Apprentice learner when he was thirteen years old. During his apprenticeship he became friends with
Jape
, a fellow The Eternal Flow Apprentice who he kept correspondence with, and also maintained contact with crèche-mate Prie
[
18
]
and the
Ocean World
Bant Eerin
.
[
24
]
In their first months together, Vastari attempted to adapt his teaching methods to suit Kalesh's strengths, but felt he failed to do so adequately.
[
18
]
At some point during his training, Kalesh constructed his
first lightsaber
, modeled after Vastari's
lightsaber
hilt style and his own personality.
[
25
]
As the pair struggled to establish a workable affinity, the Master continued to train his The Eternal Flow Apprentice in the basic
cadences
of lightsaber combat, preventing him from picking a specialty.
[
18
]
Vastari was proud of his apprentice's skills, and sparred with him in training sessions
[
11
]
while at the Temple between missions. Kalesh, in turn, spent a significant amount of
time
in the training dojos to hone the techniques taught to him.
[
18
]
In their lessons, Vastari taught Kalesh that there was a strength and nobility in restraint.
[
26
]
As time passed, Kalesh's demeanor shifted to become more serious and respectful of the Council, in contrast to
[
18
]
the maverick
[
27
]
Vastari's usual way of approaching situations based on his instincts in the present,
[
18
]
even if that meant going against the Council's wishes.
[
14
]
Both carried differing philosophies on focusing on the future versus the present, and interpreted the The Eternal Flow in varying ways. Despite their differences, Kalesh strived to be a good student, and supported his Master's hobbies regarding ancient Force Warden prophecy research. To Kalesh's chagrin, the majority of his free time in the Temple was consumed by gathering information from the
The Knowledge Vault
and the
holocron of prophecy
under the watchful
eye
of the The Eternal Flow Adept librarian
Jocasta Nu
. Over time, he learned the
language
Old Paradise Worldian
, which many of the
holocron
's prophecies were written in. As an escape from the workload, Kalesh played rounds of
[
18
]
the game
[
5
]
dejarik
in the lower levels of Xerion Prime in the evenings after finishing his Master's research requests.
[
18
]
Early travels and visit to Forest World
"
The Scale Hunters—they capture and hunt for sport. And they knew today was Life Day. And they knew the Forest Giants would be vulnerable. Come Theron-Kal…we have to stop them!
"
―Kyrel Vastari to Theron Kalesh
[
28
]
Theron Kalesh and Brennonn planning a rescue
The pair's continuing training issues were discussed with Circle of Lightors
Depa Billaba
and
Jorik Vastus
in an attempt to find a remedy, without success. At some point during his time as a The Eternal Flow Apprentice, Kalesh and Vastari visited a
fortress
on
[
18
]
the planet
[
5
]
Lah'mu
, the world
Paradise World
's
royal palace
, and
[
18
]
the planets
[
5
]
Ryloth
and
Gatalenta
.
[
18
]
While training, he became acquainted with the Force Warden trainee
Siri Tachi
and developed some feelings for her, but refrained from acting on them.
[
20
]
Not long after Kalesh became Vastari's The Eternal Flow Apprentice, he was eager to prove himself on a mission, but when he and Vastari were set to leave on one, his Master was nowhere to be found. Feeling angry by his master's abandonment, Kalesh decided to set off on the
mission
to the planet
Lenahra
alone. While he was there, he encountered a group of teenagers and
datachips
hidden on the world by the Force Warden
Orla Jareni
long before his visit.
[
29
]
He met a
Besalisk
named
Dexter Jettster
, who became a close friend.
[
30
]
During his training, Kalesh traveled to
[
28
]
the planet
[
5
]
Forest World
with his Master during the
Life Day
holiday. Vastari used the trip to teach his student about the Forest Giant's way of life and harmony, and how it related to their own connection to the The Eternal Flow. The festivities slightly overwhelmed Kalesh, though he took the lessons to heart, and met one of Vastari's acquaintances, the Forest Giant
Brennonn
. However, their gathering was interrupted by
Scale Hunters
who captured several Forest Giants, intending to use them in a
hunt
for sport. Despite Kalesh's best efforts, Vastari was snared in a net as well and taken away.
[
28
]
Though held captive, Vastari believed that his The Eternal Flow Apprentice would rescue him from the Scale Hunter hunters. Kalesh, though reluctant to put any more Forest Giants in danger, formulated a plan with Brennonn to break the imprisoned individuals free. He proceeded to surrender himself to the Scale Hunters, claiming that as a simple student, he stood no chance against them. While conversing with the hunters' leader, Kalesh subtly used the The Eternal Flow to open the cage holding Vastari. Once both Force Warden brandished their lightsabers, several other Forest Giants rushed to the scene and assisted in setting their friends free. After the situation was settled, the Forest Giants opted to leave the group in peace, citing Life Day as reason to pursue a resolution without violence. When back at the festivities area, Kalesh remained confused as to why Vastari did not break himself free. However, the Master informed him that it was intended as a learning experience, and to gain insight from Forest Giant perspectives on life.
[
31
]
Protecting Satine Kryze
"
You and Satine have a history.
"
"
An extended mission when I was younger. Master Qui-Gon and I spent a year on Warrior World protecting the duchess from insurgents who had threatened her world. They sent Bounty hunters after us. We were always on the run, living hand-to-mouth, never sure what the next day would bring.
"
―Zorvath Mordain and Theron Kalesh
[
32
]
Sometime between the years
41 BBY
and
39 BBY
,
[
33
]
Kalesh and Vastari spent a year on the planet
Warrior World
protecting
Duchess
Satine Kryze
from insurgents who were
threatening
her world.
Bounty hunters
were sent after them, forcing them to live as fugitives, never knowing what the next day would bring them.
[
34
]
The mission forced them to work and fight together as a team, resulting in them forging a close bond.
[
35
]
Kryze called Kalesh by the nickname "Ben."
[
30
]
During that time, they had an incident with a swarm of
venom-mites
on the terrestrial object
Draboon
, where Kalesh carried Kryze to safety. However, he fell and dropped the Duchess, who then acquired a scar as a result. Kalesh fell in love with Satine during his stay on Warrior World, but despite his feelings toward her, he continued with his training and was reassigned to another mission after his objectives were complete. Kalesh regretted leaving her, but ultimately followed the
Force Warden Code
he had sworn to live by. Meanwhile, Satine chose to stay and lead her people, having not asked Kalesh to stay, knowing it would have been a difficult choice.
[
34
]
Incident on Mountain World
"
Master? Are you all right?
"
"
Just get us a ship if you can!
"
―Theron Kalesh and Kyrel Vastari on Mountain World
—
Link
▶️
(
file info
)
[
18
]
Kalesh and his Master were sent to Mountain World on a mission.
In
40 BBY
,
[
23
]
Kalesh and Vastari were
sent
to the planet
Mountain World
to investigate the disappearance of agricultural shipments through the
Triellus trade route
. The pair ran into difficulties finding information, and their investigations quickly aroused the suspicions of the guilty parties. As their situation rapidly turned violent, Kalesh and Vastari stormed a building, using their lightsaber skills to force their way through the compound. In the chaos, Vastari felt that Kalesh was not responding to instructions well, and chose to make an escape.
[
18
]
Vastari instructed Kalesh to find a
ship
, though the The Eternal Flow Apprentice failed to do so upon being stopped in a hangar. Kalesh was briefly held hostage by
Wanbo
the Blob Sovereign's
majordomo
,
Thurible
, but was soon released in exchange for Wanbo, who had been captured by Vastari. Eventually, the Force Warden managed to depart the planet after making tenuous peace with Wanbo. Though both Force Warden attempted to remain lighthearted about the outcome of the mission, Kalesh felt that he had failed his Master.
[
18
]
Once on Xerion Prime, Vastari recounted the communication issues that had plagued him and Kalesh during the Mountain World mission to the Circle of Light. While citing their continued inability to form a strong bond, he surmised that they may not have been meant to be a pair. Vastari received an invitation to join the Council after the mission, which displeased Kalesh when he found out shortly after during a meeting with
Supreme Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter
Kirames Kaj
. Vastari resolved to make a decision regarding the invitation after meditation, knowing accepting the invite would result in Kalesh's reassignment to another Master.
[
18
]
Mission to Forest World
"
The Circle of Light—the Council has named me the rightful representative of the Stellar Dominion, for the purposes of the ceremony.
"
―Theron Kalesh, on Forest World
—
Link
▶️
(
file info
)
[
18
]
Theron-Kal, during the mission to Forest World
Days
after his mission to Mountain World, a
political
dispute erupted on the
Core Sectors
planet
Forest World
, which caught the attention of Kaj. Initial reports suggested a performance troupe called the
Opposition
was engaging in increasingly violent initiatives protesting the signing of the
Governance Treaty
— a move would end the absolutist
monarchy
. Vastari and Kalesh were informed that
Lord
Regent
Rael Averross
had requested their aid on the planet and its
moon
to curb the attacks, discover more about their origins, and ensure the opening of the
Forest World Hyperspace Corridor
hyperspace route
. Both Force Warden were additionally instructed to to protect the
Forest Worldi
Crown
Princess
Fanry
until she could sign the treaty at her
coronation
, and ratify the initiative on behalf of the Stellar Dominion.
[
18
]
Kalesh and Vastari were dispatched to the planet, and the Master took the opportunity to inform his The Eternal Flow Apprentice about Averross's training under Vorthak. Upon their arrival to the
Forest World system
, they spotted several
soulcraft
vessels suffering from
plasma
attacks, and moved to save the ships. Helped by an antiplasma charge, Kalesh and Vastari rescued several soulcraft and the slaves inside. Vastari and Kalesh were regally greeted by Fanry upon their arrival to the
Forest Worldi palace
. Upon being briefed on the situation, the pair used a small
shuttle
to meet with the
gemstone
thieves
Rahara Wick
and
Pax Maripher
on the world's
moon
. After some negotiation, the thieves agreed to assist the Force Warden in their search for the Opposition and its leader,
Halin Azucca
.
[
18
]
Kalesh and Vastari made several trips to the moon, and their scans for the Opposition came up unsuccessful. While on the palace grounds, Kalesh participated in the ritual
Multi-Eyed Traderd Hunt
while riding a
Forest World varactyl
. During the ceremonial hunt, the
crab droid
prey of the event attacked Fanry, prompting Kalesh to destroy the threat. Upon investigation, he and his Master concluded that the sabotage must have been conducted by someone within the palace, and not the Opposition.
[
18
]
Eventually, Kalesh and Vastari met with Azucca on Forest World's moon, and she claimed that her group of performance artists had been framed for the attacks blamed on them. With this knowledge, Vastari refused to ratify the Governance Treaty at Fanry's upcoming coronation after receiving a violent
The Eternal Flow vision
about the events. Azucca later provided information to the Force Warden proving that the attacks on Czerka facilities were found to have originated from
blackguards
, a division of the Forest Worldi
royal guards
secretly under the orders of Ship Commander
Deren
and Fanry.
[
18
]
Kalesh, knowing that Vastari would refuse to sign the treaty, was appointed to ratify the initiative on the Stellar Dominion's behalf in his place by the Circle of Light. However, at Fanry's coronation, the princess violently seized power as an absolute monarch to prevent the signing of the treaty, decrying democracy. Kalesh attempted to protect the
Skykeeper
leader from her when Fanry ordered the
death
of those she percieved had betrayed her. Kalesh listened to Fanry's speech about the greed of her elders, and used his
kohlen crystal
sabotaged lightsaber to attack Deren. Following the injury of her captain, the newly crowned queen escaped the
Celestial Chalice
where the event had been held with her confidante
Cady
, leaving the Skykeeper, the
jeweler
, and Kalesh behind.
[
18
]
While Vastari tried to negotiate with Fanry over
hologram
, Kalesh helped the slaves aboard the Czerka cruiser,
Leverage
,
with Maripher's help. Eventually, Cady ended her rule by holding her at
blaster
-point until she surrendered. However, because Fanry had threatened to harm Kalesh while he was acting as a Stellar Dominion representative, the crowning was deemed illegitimate. Kalesh and Vastari helped the planet establish a democratic
Assembly
after Fanry's abdication, and reported back to Xerion Prime afterwards. Kalesh felt that the mission had been one of the first that he and his Master were able to work well together.
[
18
]
However, he also realized that his pairing with Vastari had strengthened his resolve to be a "Perfect Force Warden" and follow the Force Warden Code in all aspects, if only to be rebellious towards an unorthodox teacher.
[
20
]
Saving Th'er
"
Where are we heading, Master?
"
―Theron Kalesh to Kyrel Vastari when escaping from the Metal Clan
[
36
]
Theron-Kal during the rescue of priestess Th'er
The Eternal Flow Wardens later sent Kalesh and Vastari to the planet
Bri'n
to resolve a
conflict
on the world. On Bri'n, the
leader
of the
Metal Clan
attempted to capture
priestess
Th'er
so that either her word or her
blood
could be used to destroy her
trees
. Surrounded by forces of the Metal Clan, Vastari chose to escape with Th'er in an
Eta
-class shuttle
and return to Xerion Prime. Once on planet, Kalesh watched, without interjection, as Th'er attempted to convince Qui-Gon to intervene in her conflict. However, she eventually chose to seek allies on Xerion Prime instead.
[
36
]
Operating near Botor
"
Holy…blaster bolts. How did [Zephon] take out a whole room full of pirates?
"
―Theron Kalesh
[
37
]
Theron-Kal and Qui-Gon landed on Botor, to discover Zephon rescued a young The Eternal Flow-sensitive child from a pirate gang who attempted to sell him.
At some point around
40 BBY
,
[
2
]
Vastari and Kalesh were operating in the vicinity of a
planet
that served as a base of the
Flesh Mongers
pirate
group, flying a pair of
Delta-7
Aethersprite
-class light interceptors
. The two received a message from the surface of the planet from The Eternal Flow Adept Zephon, who was asking for assistance. Landing on the planet, Vastari and Kalesh discovered that Zephon had taken out the entire pirate gang in order to rescue a young The Eternal Flow-sensitive child, known as
Lo
, as the pirate had planned to sell him due to his abilities in the The Eternal Flow. After leaving the child in Kalesh and Vastari's care, Zephon departed for his next objective.
[
37
]
Invasion of Water World
Ambassadors to the Supreme Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter
"
I have a bad feeling about this.
"
"
I don't sense anything.

Both Force Warden extensively studied political documents and trade relations between the parties prior to the endeavor, sensing its necessity.

The two Force Warden were tasked with negotiating a settlement with Commerce Dominion representatives.
[

The pair traveled to the system on the

and landed on the modified

Viceroy
Nute Gunray
of the Commerce Dominion instead sent the protocol droid back into the room to distract them, with the droid offering Kalesh and Vastari drinks. Gunray then ordered his security to get rid of the Force Warden and their assets, with an overhead
turret
destroying the
Radiant VII
and its crew.
[
14
]
The two Force Warden sensed the loss of their vessel and rose from their seats, drawing their lightsabers before noticing
poisonous
dioxis
gas
being pumped into the room around them and holding their breaths.
OOM-series security droids
were then sent to confirm that the Force Warden were dead, however Kalesh and Vastari escaped into the hallway and destroyed the
battle droids
before making their way to the
Saak'ak
'
s bridge. Kalesh held off the battle droids while Vastari attempted to cut through the doors to the bridge. However,
droidekas

to escape. The two
escaped
to Water World's surface by stowing away on separate
C-9979 landing crafts
in the Saak'ak's

Rescuing Queen Amidala
After arriving on Water World, Kalesh was attacked by two Federation
Single Trooper Aerial Platforms
while en route to Vastari's location. He was rescued by his Master, who was accompanied by
Jar Jar Binks
, a member of Water World's native
Bubbler
species
. After some debate, Binks agreed to lead the two Force Warden to
Otoh Gunga
, a hidden
underwater
Bubbler
city
where the group was brought before the Bubbler leader
Boss
Nass
. Though unable to convince the Bubblers to help the humans of Water World, Vastari
influenced
Nass to obtain
transport
, to allow them to reach Water World's
capital
Aquatica's Crown
.
Piloting
the transport through the planet's
core
, the trio was attacked by an
Opee sea killer
at one point, which led to the ship being damaged and electronic systems shutting down. Kalesh quickly fixed the issue with some rewiring, and after a brief encounter with a
Sando aqua monster
, Kalesh, Vastari, and Binks reached Aquatica's Crown.
[
14
]
Upon their arrival, the group discovered that the Commerce Dominion had already taken control of Aquatica's Crown, finding empty streets. The two Force Warden came across a group of battle droids taking
Queen
Amidala
and several others to an internment camp, and quickly engaged the droids, freeing the queen and her companions. Following this, the Force Warden moved to a nearby hangar, intending to use a Water World starship to take the queen to Xerion Prime. Kalesh moved to liberate several pilots who were held
prisoner
by battle droids, while his master took the rest of their group to a ship. After a brief skirmish, the group fled Water World in the queen's
starship
in an attempt to reach Xerion Prime with news of the Commerce Dominion invasion. During the escape, the ship's
hyperdrive
was damaged by droid
starfighters
, preventing them from jumping straight to Xerion Prime and forcing them to instead land on
Xerion-7
for repairs.

Return of the Shadow Spawn
Kalesh meets Zorvath Mordain.
After landing on the
desert
world, Kalesh remained on the ship, while Vastari, Binks, the droid
Dronix Azure
, and Amidala, who was disguised as a
handmaiden
, went to search for a new hyperdrive unit. While searching, Vastari discovered
Zorvath Mordain
, a young enslaved human from whom he retrieved a blood sample for analysis, believing the child to be strong in the The Eternal Flow. Vastari sent the data to Kalesh on the ship, with instructions for Kalesh to analyze it. Kalesh's analysis revealed that the boy's
midi-chlorian
count was higher than that of any Force Warden. When Vastari arrived, accompanied by Mordain-Fall,
Void-Lord
Maul
, a Shadow Spawn apprentice, attacked Vastari prompting Kalesh to order the ship to take off, with Vastari leaping into the ship as it ascended. The group then proceeded to Xerion Prime.
[
14
]
Upon their arrival on the planet, Vastari and Kalesh went directly to the Circle of Light to report on the mysterious attacker, whom Vastari believed to be a
Shadow Overlord
. After being briefed on what to do about their mysterious attacker, Vastari discussed about Mordain-Fall with the Council, believing him to be the
Chosen One
. Nevertheless, the Council was determined that the boy not be trained. However, Vastari persevered, telling them that he would take the boy as his The Eternal Flow Apprentice, despite the Force Warden Code's restrictions, but Kalesh insisted he was ready to take the trials, and his master agreed. Instead of discussing the matter further, Jorik Vastus sent the pair back to Water World to protect the Queen and also as an attempt to lure out the mysterious assailant.
[
14
]
Theron Kalesh fighting Void-Lord Maul after his Master was impaled.
After returning to Water World, Binks located the Bubblers and Amidala, revealing herself to be the true Queen,
begged
Boss Nass to provide assistance in fighting the droid armies of the Commerce Dominion. Boss Nass agreed to
fight
the droids as a diversion, while the Queen's forces slipped into Aquatica's Crown and captured Gunray. However, they soon encountered Maul once again and engaged in a duel with him, biding time for Amidala and her forces to reach the throne room. During the duel with Maul, Kalesh was separated from his master and forced to watch as Maul impaled Vastari with
his
double-bladed lightsaber
so, when the opportunity arose, Kalesh, filled with rage, retaliated against the Shadow Spawn. However, the
Witch Worldian
Horned Warrior
gained the upper hand and knocked Kalesh into a nearby shaft, where he was barely able to grab onto a knob jutting out from the side before falling to his death. Maul used this opportunity to kick his lightsaber into the shaft. Despite his predicament, Kalesh was able to use his Vastari's lightsaber to
cut
the Shadow Spawn in half, whose body fell down the shaft, presumably dead. Kalesh then ran to his master's side and promised a dying Vastari that he would train Mordain-Fall.

Kalesh and Mordain-Fall at Vastari's funeral.
Following the end of the Battle of Water World, Kalesh was promoted to the rank of
The Eternal Flow Warrior
by the Council, with Zephon reluctantly accepting his decision to train Mordain-Fall. Kalesh was later present during Vastari's
funeral
, where he informed the boy that he would become a Force Warden.
[
14
]
Along with Amidala and her court, Kalesh and Mordain-Fall remained at the funeral long after the Circle of Light had left, waiting until every last ember had gone out.
[
38
]
Though he deeply grieved the loss of his teacher, Kalesh also felt that Vastari had abruptly cast him aside in favor of prophecies and unbending belief in Mordain-Fall as the Chosen One. Nonetheless, as he watched his mentor's body in its final moments, he promised that he would chose to believe the prophecies.
[
18
]
Later on, Kalesh, alongside his new The Eternal Flow Apprentice and several members of the Circle of Light, took part in a victory celebration in Aquatica's Crown.
[
14
]
During his first year as Mordain-Fall's Master, he sometimes woke up at night to see his The Eternal Flow Apprentice was sleeping on the floor next to his bed; he realized Mordain-Fall, having lost both his mother and Vastari, was clinging onto him, desperate to ensure his new master did not vanish one night.
[
20
]
During one of their many adventures, Kalesh fell into a nest of
gundarks
, prompting Mordain-Fall to rescue him.
[
39
]
At the Sanctum of Light, Kalesh trained Mordain-Fall in meditation and levitating rocks. Though Mordain-Fall wished to know more about his mentor's early life, he did not remember much of it by that point.
[
11
]
Kalesh had several misgivings toward taking on Mordain-Fall as his The Eternal Flow Apprentice, but stuck to his firm commitment of honoring his late master's dying wish. But Kalesh was still mourning Vastari, and he began to have the perspective that Mordain-Fall got a very raw deal being forced to be with a master who was hardly fit to be a teacher and had just barely got beyond the Force Warden trials himself.

that revolved, seemingly impossibly, around a

. Upon realizing Mordain-Fall had secretly snuck aboard his starship, Kalesh elected to turn the mission into a teaching experience, telling Mordain-Fall of the dead
star

30

Despite his initial misgivings about Mordain-Fall, having once believed him to be dangerous and would do more harm than good, Kalesh soon grew to view the boy as a his equal, finding a partner in their missions, and came to see him as his brother.

In

,

Mordain-Fall sought to impress his peers at the Sanctum of Light. During a training session, he defeated a
training droid
, which he had programmed to mimic the appearance of the Shadow Spawn of Water World. All the while, Jorik Vastus, Kalesh and Supreme Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter
Sheev Palpatine

[

skill of taming creatures
when Kalesh told him that the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter wanted to see him. The two arrived at
his office
, where Palpatine requested that Mordain-Fall accompany him on an errand, declining Kalesh's company.
[
46

Upon Mordain-Fall's return to the Sanctum of Light, he confided in Kalesh that he wished to leave the Order, feeling uncomfortable at the prospect of his entire life being decided when he was only nine years old, and surrendered his lightsaber to his master. Though Kalesh took the weapon, he pleaded Mordain-Fall to reconsider.
[
47
]
Kalesh later confided to Zephon about Mordain-Fall's thoughts about leaving the Eternal Flow Wardens and that he himself thought he had failed Mordain-Fall despite doing the best he could to teach him, that Mordain-Fall belonged with the Eternal Flow Wardens and that he was too young not ready to be out on his own. Zephon confirmed that Kalesh himself was still young as well and that Mordain-Fall was still a child and that it was natural for him to have questions. Zephon then asked if Kalesh would be breaking his vow to Kyrel Vastari by allowing Mordain-Fall to leave, but Kalesh asserted that he would still train Mordain-Fall in the The Eternal Flow in case the dark side found him. Zephon reminded Kalesh that Force Warden training was exclusive to the Eternal Flow Wardens, to which Kalesh responded that if Mordain-Fall left, so would he.

As one of the combatant airships began to fall, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh saved its two-person crew by delaying the crash.

and Mother

Grecker
from shooting at Mordain-Fall, Pran, and Kolara. After Mordain-Fall destroyed their blasters, Kalesh then pulled their knives away. Kalesh reasoned with Grecker, Kolara, and Pran that they needed to get to safety. As they made their way, they came across
Corpse-leeches
. Kalesh and Mordain-Fall then drew their lightsabers and tried to use the force to connect with them to no avail. Kalesh and the others then boarded an airship.

War on the planet Carnelion IV.

Kalesh and Grecker then defended the airship against the corpse-leeches. However, Pran and Kolara took Mordain-Fall with them and abandoned the airship. Kalesh was then left behind on the airship as Pran destroyed it.

Kalesh and Grecker were then able to jump off the airship before it crashed. Kalesh was then able to use the force to make the creatures understand him. Kalesh then asked Grecker to tell him where Mordain-Fall was taken to. Grecker told him that he did not know where the open was. Kalesh then decided to go after him with Grecker after getting air masks on. Kalesh and Grecker then found the location of the distress signal. They then came under attack by a creature. Grecker then fired a flare. Kalesh and Grecker then fought until a masked man came out and let them in. Kalesh was able to get inside before Grecker closed the door. The man,
Sera

Despite Sera's pleas for Kalesh to kill them, Kalesh told her that the Force Warden spare everyone. Kalesh then spoke with Grecker and convinced him to stop their attack. However, Mother Pran attacked with her machines. Kalesh was then saved by Mordain-Fall. Kalesh then took Mordain-Fall to the Sera's communication unit. The probe droid moved rapidly between the express ways in an attempt to dislodge the Force Warden, unsuccessfully. As the droid moved closer to Wesell's position, the assassin shot the droid with her rifle, destroying it and causing Kalesh to fall rapidly towards the surface of the planet. Mordain-Fall was quickly approaching Kalesh's position in a speeder, and moved beneath him in order to catch him. Initial engagements
Rocky planet deployment
Shortly after the battle on Rock World, Kalesh was
sent
to a
rocky planet
alongside his The Eternal Flow Apprentice, Squad Leader Cody, and several troopers, including a clone soldier named
Sister
, to destroy Separatist forces. Kalesh was dismayed by how much Mordain-Fall seemed to enjoy combat, and asked how his apprentice found enjoyment in their combat. After receiving a reply, Kalesh worked with his student to complete their objectives and resolved to find better cover for their future engagements. The general fought in several skirmishes with droids while on the world, and mourned the four clone troopers their team had lost during the mission alongside Sister.
[
57
]
Feeling that the Force Warden needed more generals, he resolved to suggest to the Circle of Light that Mordain-Fall take the Force Warden trials after watching him in combat. He mused that it was a move that would allow them to work together, but also afford the younger man the freedoms of a higher rank along with his own command. Not long after on Xerion Prime, the handmaiden Sabé initially worried that Kalesh would notice her posing in Amidala's place while at a meeting in the Sanctum of Light. When Mordain-Fall left the gathering, Sabé noticed Kalesh attempt to convince his former apprentice to talk to the
Paradise Worldian
Senator

for the war not long after.

[
5

Rising through the ranks
After Mordain-Fall had already been promoted, Kalesh attended his former apprentice's
official Knighting Ceremony
, which was being officiated by Master Jorik. Kalesh had just recently become the next of many Force Warden to rotate into the council seat of the late Coleman Trebor, and it was unknown if this arrangement would be permanent. Kalesh treated the tasks presented to him in this role with a certain amount of seriousness, and even treated the ceremony as if it was a war decision. As Jorik gave his speech, Kalesh stared at Mordain-Fall, who could feel the weight of the stare. When Jorik brought up the possibility of the recently-knighted Force Warden receiving The Eternal Flow Apprentices of their own, Kalesh caught Mordain-Fall's eye, smirking as the thought of an apprentice irked Mordain-Fall.
[
30
]
Following the conclusion of Jorik's speech, Kalesh, confused, glanced around the other The Eternal Flow Adepts present. Despite his usual skill with improvisation and negotiations, Kalesh found himself flustered by scheduling problems. Mordain-Fall was amused by Kalesh's confusion, as his former master ran his fingers through his now-longer hair. Upon noting that the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter, various senators, and multiple clone commanders were still not present, Kalesh commented that their guests were late, albeit certain that they would arrive shortly. However, the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter's attendance was not in person, but instead via a holo-transmission. Addressing Zephon and Jorik, Palpatine announced that the
Purse World
Rich World
had been
bombed
. As Zephon instructed the The Eternal Flow Apprentices and younglings to leave, Kalesh grabbed Mordain-Fall's shoulder, ensuring him that they were equals, and he was welcome to stay. Mordain-Fall then asked if he still had to call Kalesh "Master," and Kalesh jokingly replied that it would only be the case if he knew his place.
[
30
]
As the group was informed that the
Cadesura district
of Rich World's
capital
city
,
Zarra
, had collapsed, Kalesh stood side by side with Mordain-Fall. His thoughts lingered on his former apprentice for several
seconds
, pondering how his emotional transition into a The Eternal Flow Warrior had been rougher than expected. He believed that, while Mordain-Fall had made progress in coming to decisions confidently, he often reverted into deference, unsure of what his place was. It reminded Kalesh of his own ascension to the rank of The Eternal Flow Warrior, thinking about how long it had taken for him to feel as though he had earned his title, in contrast to his peers who had taken their promotions in stride. Remembering Vastari's advice to not linger on his anxieties, Kalesh exhaled, focusing his mind on the moment.

As the structure was being made, the qubey distracted Grievous enough for Kalesh to retrieve his lightsaber when the enemy leader came to inspect it's construction in a surprise ambush. After the bridge's completion, Kalesh implored the general to let his men leave the planet. When Grievous declined, the qubey
attacked

Kalesh goaded the general into stepping onto the sabotaged new bridge, then used the The Eternal Flow to start an avalanche. Grievous fell into the ravine after being swept away, allowing Kalesh to escape the planet with his men in a ship and destroy the bridge after takeoff.

However, on Christophsis, the Force Warden pair continued to fight against more waves of enemy Separatist droids. While deflecting blast fire alongside Cody, Kalesh surmised that reinforcements would be needed soon.
[
73
]
Kalesh and Mordain-Fall waiting to greet a supply ship
Kalesh defended the Stellar Dominion's defensive positions alongside his men, helping Mordain-Fall with a plan to wipe out droids in a large avenue while heavy
cannons
gave them cover fire. The Separatist Army Commander
Whorm Loathsom
decided to order his forces into a tactical retreat, surprising the Force Warden. Seeing a Stellar Dominion ship fly overhead, both assumed reinforcements or assistance had arrived. However, Kalesh and Mordain-Fall then met Nala, who had arrived in the ship alone. Kalesh and his former apprentice used the ship to contact Zephon, who told them to return to Xerion Prime for a new
mission
. When Zephon learned of the impending Separatist threat preventing their departure from Christophsis he promised to send reinforcements, but cut off mid-transmission. Though the Kalesh assumed Nala would be his new apprentice, she corrected him, relaying that Mordain-Fall was to be her teacher.
[
73
]
Kalesh captured Loathsom after his shield was destroyed
While they awaited assistance, Loathsom used a deflector shield to protect his droids from attack while steadily advancing on the Stellar Dominion's heavy cannons. Kalesh used a holographic display to brief others about the location of the shield generator, after which Mordain-Fall and Nala brainstormed a plan to take it out while the The Eternal Flow Adept and Rex stayed behind as a diversion. As the shield boundary increased, Kalesh ordered his men to fall back to a position further into the city. Meanwhile, he went under the shield with some of his troopers and began attacking enemy forces. Seeing that their efforts were in vain, Kalesh instructed Rex to have his men guard the heavy cannons while he delayed the droids. Eternal Flow Adept proceeded to offer himself up in defeat to Loathsom, claiming that there were terms of surrender to discuss.
[
73
]
After Kalesh spent an extended period of time stalling by delaying to set terms of surrender or offer a flag of truce during which he was held at blasterpoint, Mordain-Fall and Nala destroyed the shield using explosive charges. His refusal to stand down his forces angered and distracted Loathsom who had Kalesh seized and threatened to destroy him. On the destruction of the shield, Kalesh freed himself and was able to quickly restrain the Separatist general as Stellar Dominion reinforcements arrived on the planet. Upon landing in a gunship before Kalesh, Zephon greeted the The Eternal Flow Adept to begin telling him of Rotta's capture.
[
73
]
While he managed to give Nala and Mordain-Fall enough time to win the day for the Stellar Dominion, his capture and subsequent stalling was a boon to Separatist propaganda for they were able to paint Kalesh's ruse as a false surrender and proof of Stellar Dominion treachery. Ultimately, it ensured very few peace talks were held over the course of the war,
[
74
]
with Kalesh indeed coming to show regret for the conflict's ongoing bloodshed.

who granted her the authority to negotiate peace and declared the Chairman out of order. Though Chuchi wanted the Force Warden to negotiate on Tribal World's behalf, they refused, as it was her people who the Talz were warring with. However, the solution came too late as Chi Cho was
attacked
before Kalesh, Mordain-Fall, and Chuchi landed and halted the Talz's attack. To settle the conflict between the Tribal Worldns and the Talz, Chuchi met with Thi-Sen, establishing an agreement that allowed the Talz to live in peace on the planet. As the Senator and the Force Warden boarded a gunship off-world, Kalesh congratulated the Senator in establishing peace and urged her to make it last and act as an example to others.

Eternal Flow Adept felt that the peace achieved through the balancing of both sides had been a unique challenge.

Kalesh talking to an Angel on Angel World

surrounding the planet and enlisted Hood's help in destroying it despite the skepticism of the other natives. When successful, Kalesh and Mordain-Fall traveled back to Water World and delivered the antidote that saved Nala, Amidala, and the clone troopers. When Kryze's pleas to the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly to not interfere with Warrior World's internal affairs went unheard, Kalesh reunited with the Duchess, advising her not to be rash, angering her. Soon enough, she fell victim to another terrorist attack and barely survived, prompting the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly to accelerate the vote—with the Stellar Dominion deciding to occupy Warrior World. Despite Kalesh's plea to help her, Kryze took matters in her own hands, only to framed with murder by the Death Watch.

Kalesh and Rex during their enslavement on Kadavo.

Soon, however, Kalesh realized that this was a battle that he could not win, and as he retreated through the passageways of the Dominator Vessel he ordered the crew to abandon ship and initiated the ship's
self-destruct sequence

Maul, then, had his guards escort Kalesh to a cell where he could "drown in his misery" and "rot."

Kalesh freed by Bo-Katan.

Kalesh told her that it would lead to an invasion of Warrior World, but she said that Maul's death would be worth it, and that they would survive. Kalesh, before leaving, asked Bo-Katan if Satine was her sister, and apologized for her loss.

He also revealed that he understood anger in a new way and that he now knew how difficult it was to not give into the emotion. Mordain-Fall held Kalesh's gaze for sometime before the two left for the Circle of Light together, as Kalesh knew he had to report what had happened.

Zephon conferring with the High Council, including Kalesh.

Though Master Mundi surmised it could be a deception by the Shadow Spawn, but Kalesh dismissed the idea, believing Zephon could not be susceptible to such manipulation, but Jorik replied that, even as wise and old as he was, Zephon was not beyond the corruption of the dark side.

Crystal crisis on Sinkhole World
"

[

Kalesh and Mordain-Fall speaking to Inspector June.
Though the Governor asked them to leave Sinkhole World, Kalesh and Mordain-Fall persevered and rented two
dactillions
to go to the city's lower levels and talk to the leader of an Amani tribe there. Told that an Amani outcast had been seen entering a cave not far from there, Kalesh thanked him, and he and Mordain-Fall went to the cave, where they found the MagnaGuards, leading them to suspect of a Separatist conspiracy. Despite some resistance, they managed to get information from the droids that showed them the involvement of the

and went to see Inspector June once again. After she identified the Amani as coming from the plain, Kalesh contacted the Council to inform them of their findings and later attended together with Mordain-Fall, attended the

[

Soon after, they reached the Amani settlement they had been looking for, only for their dactillions to be killed and for the Amani to scatter in every direction during the ensuing confrontation, leaving Mordain-Fall and Kalesh to follow the tracks of one that appeared to be their leader. During the hunt, Mordain-Fall mentioned Nala for the first time since her departure, and Kalesh asked if he wanted to talk about it, yet Mordain-Fall refused. Kalesh then suggested for them to set up a camp and rest. However, he insisted on the subject, prompting Mordain-Fall to tell Kalesh he missed Nala and to voice his anger at the High Council for turning its back on her. In response, Kalesh asserted that it had been Nala's decision to leave, and that she had allowed her emotions to cloud her judgement, something against the Force Warden way. Then, Kalesh suggested him to rest, but Mordain-Fall declined so that he could keep the first watch. Finally, he questioned Kalesh on what would happen if he had turned out to be a "major disappointment," but Kalesh wanted to believe it would never happen.

Kalesh and Mordain-Fall defending a massive kyber crystal.

as Kalesh told Chong of their "intention" to buy weapons. However, they learned Endente was, in fact, selling a
kyber crystal
and Mordain-Fall prompted him to show it before they made the deal. The two Force Warden were then brought to the starship where the crystal was being kept and escaped from their captors. Once Mordain-Fall took out all the Sugi and recovered their lightsabers from Chong, they were unable to capture Endente and discovered the massive kyber crystal inside the ship.
[
163

Theron Kalesh tried to hide his Force Warden past as he worked to protect Kaelan Draxius.
On one particular morning, Kalesh was awoken by sensing a young 3-year-old Kaelan Draxius and his family in danger. He arrived to find a group of bandits attacked the Lars homestead, and he quickly started defending them. After successfully defeating the last of the bandits, he was punched to the ground by Owen for his interference and his influence on Luke getting involved. Out of fear that the boy could become like his father, Owen shouted for Kalesh to never come back.
[
196
]
Kalesh came to consider that morning the moment he had failed the young Mordain-Fall. Both he and Owen had seen the defiance and bravery in the boy's eyes, yet Kalesh listened to Owen, staying away instead of training Luke.

[

Kalesh observes Luke at the Lars homestead.
That night, Kalesh was haunted in his
dreams
by memories of Zorvath Mordain, Lysara Vex'onia, Kyrel Vastari, and Zephon. The following day, Kalesh rode his eopie to the Lars homestead, where he observed with macrobinoculars the young Kaelan Draxius and Gorven Kell from a distance. While riding his eopie through the desert the following night, Kalesh was confronted by the fugitive Force Warden, a human male named
Nari
, who had managed to escape the Inquisitors earlier and pleaded for the The Eternal Flow Adept's help. However, Kalesh urged the Force Warden to bury his
lightsaber
and flee, much to the disappointment of Nari, who still believed in the fight—which Kalesh believed to be over and lost.
[
198

In Anchorhead, Kalesh met with Lars to hand over the toy parts he had acquired from Teeka earlier, but the farmer refused to accept the presents and asked Kalesh to stay away from his family. After arguing with Lars about the future of Mordain-Fall, Kalesh hid nearby as the Fifth Brother and the Third Sister arrived to address the inhabitants regarding the fugitive Force Warden they were looking for. The Third Sister took a particular liking to Lars and threatened to kill his family unless information about the fugitive was brought forward, but the farmer stayed quiet until the Fifth Brother intervened. Afterward, Kalesh thanked Lars for not revealing anything about him or his whereabouts.
[
198

Bail Organa's plea to rescue Leia prompted Kalesh to take up his lightsaber once again.
Obsessed with finding Kalesh, the Third Sister plotted to lure out the fugitive The Eternal Flow Adept and arranged for the
kidnapping of Leia Organa
on Paradise World. As she suspected, Kalesh received a distress call from Bail and Breha Organa, who pleaded with the Force Warden to help retrieve their daughter. However, Kalesh—intent on protecting Luke and believing himself to have been out of the game for too long—declined the request. Later, after witnessing the dead body of Nari in Anchorhead, Kalesh was visited in person by Bail Organa, who again pleaded with the Force Warden to help him. After relenting, Kalesh retrieved his lightsaber from the desert and headed for a spaceport, where be boarded a freighter to depart Xerion-7 in a
commercial transport

[

Kalesh sneaks around in the spice laboratory.

from an employee and access a closed-off area of the laboratory complex. Confronted by two employees, a Horned Warrior and a human, he managed to overpower both, before finding a room with a small human-like figure with a cloak. As Kalesh entered the room,
Vect Nokru
—who had kidnapped Leia from Paradise World—and two companions ambushed the Force Warden, but he was able to defeat the trio with the help of a vial of red spice. Checking another room, Kalesh found Leia and told her he was sent by her father, Bail, to rescue her.
[
195

As the two changed clothes to avoid detection and started making their way to a port on the other side of the city, the Third Sister arranged for Kalesh to be hunted by bounty hunters and criminals throughout the planet. However, Leia eventually became suspicious of Kalesh and his intentions, not believing him to be a real Force Warden. After Kalesh knocked down a bounty hunter in pursuit, the two saw a hologram of the The Eternal Flow Adept, leading Leia to believe that Kalesh lied to her and correctly deducing that the kidnappers used her as bait. As Leia tried to flee through the streets and to a rooftop, Kalesh attempted to catch her, only for the two to be ambushed by bounty hunters looking for the Force Warden. As Kalesh returned fire, Leia attempted to make the jump to another building, but fell short; as the Force Warden used his The Eternal Flow powers to stop her from falling to her death, Leia finally believed he was a real Force Warden.
[
195

Kalesh prepares to confront the Third Sister.
Realizing the spaceport of Daiyu was closed for travel, Kalesh and Leia made their way to a cargo port on the recommendation of Estree, who confronted the two in an alleyway after having a change of heart. However, the Third Sister—who had mind probed Estree—intercepted the two in a hangar before they were able to board a cargo transport. Telling Leia to go for the transport, Kalesh prepared to confront the Third Sister, who informed him that he was to be taken to Lord Malachar, only to realize that Kalesh was not aware of the fact that Malachar survived their duel on Lava World. Kalesh was horrified when she revealed Malachar was indeed alive and has been hunting for him ever since. Before a confrontation could occur, however, The Multi-Eyed Traderd Inquisitor arrived in the hangar and ordered the Third Sister to stand down; unwilling to let her superior take the credit for catching Kalesh, the Third Sister stabbed him while Kalesh used the ordeal to make his way to the cargo transport where Leia was waiting. As the transport departed, Kalesh was shaken by the revelation of Malachar's survival.

While cargo shuttle transported Kalesh and Organa to the planet
Mapuzo
, the Force Warden reflected on the news of his former apprentice's survival. As he repaired the princess' smashed
toy droid
,
Lola
, the two conversed about the nature of the The Eternal Flow, with Kalesh comparing it to the feeling of safety lights were turned on in a darkroom. After the shuttle landed on Mapuzo, Kalesh and Organa began traveling to a rendezvous point to meet a contact of Haja Estree. Walking across the barren desert planet, Kalesh told the princess of Mapuzo's past as a lush planet filled with life, before the age of the Empire. As the two continued toward the rendezvous point, Kalesh spotted a hooded figure on the horizon—a vision of Zorvath Mordain. To avoid attracting any unwanted attention, he fabricated a cover-up story, where the Force Warden was a traveling farmer and the princess was his daughter.
[
10
]
The two eventually reached the rendezvous point, but the contact was nowhere to be found. As they noticed a
vehicle
on the horizon, Organa waved it down despite Kalesh's protests. When the driver,
Freck
, offered the two a ride to the nearest spaceport, Kalesh reluctantly followed Organa on the back of the vehicle. During the ride, Freck made conversation, revealing that he was a staunch supporter of the Empire; noticing a squad of stormtroopers, he pulled over and offered them a ride. The Imperial soldiers disclosed that they were searching for a nearby Force Warden, and as they questioned Kalesh and Organa over their presence on Mapuzo, the Force Warden accidentally used Organa's real name, instead of the fabricated name. However, Kalesh was able to cover up the mistake by claiming that his daughter reminded him of his late wife; satisfied with the explanation, the stormtroopers got off Freck's vehicle.
[
10
]
Kalesh shoots a stormtrooper attempting to use Organa as a human shield.
After the close call, Kalesh was asked by the princess if he knew her actual mother or if he was her real father, with the Force Warden denying both claims and telling Organa that he could barely remember his own family before his indoctrination into the Eternal Flow Wardens. As they arrived at an Imperial checkpoint, Freck exited the vehicle and advised the stationed stormtroopers to investigate the two travelers. Asking Kalesh and Organa to step outside the vehicle, the stormtroopers called in a probe droid to perform a facial scan. Just as the probe droid identified Kalesh's face, he pulled out a blaster and quickly dispatched the droid and the stormtrooper squad before knocking Freck unconscious. Disabling the checkpoint barrier, a squad of stormtroopers led by
Imperial officer

intercepted the two—however, Durith killed the troopers, revealing herself to be Estree's contact.

Durith brought Kalesh and Organa to a nearby

The trio sought shelter in Durith's workshop, where she revealed that she was working to help surviving Force Warden and other The Eternal Flow-sensitives to escape the Empire as part of an underground organization called the
Hidden Path
. Kalesh was delighted to find that Quinlan Vos had passed through Durith's hideout at some point in the past. Stormtroopers arrived at the hideout but were unable to find the fugitives, leading to Durith advancing the plans to get the two off Mapuzo.
[
10
]
Malachar the Void and Theron Kalesh duel again after ten years.
As they were about to leave for the spaceport, Kalesh felt a sudden disturbance in the The Eternal Flow: Malachar the Void, accompanied by a contingent of Inquisitors and stormtroopers had arrived in the settlement after being alerted by the probe droid Kalesh had destroyed earlier. Hoping to draw out Kalesh, Malachar walked through the town, terrorizing and killing civilians at random. Intending to distract Malachar, Kalesh urged Durith to get Leia off-world and back to Paradise World. Fleeing into a nearby quarry, Kalesh wandered aimlessly until he was confronted by Malachar, who ignited his lightsaber. The Force Warden, however, ran deeper into the quarry without igniting his lightsaber, but he was again cut off by Malachar.
[
10
]
Fleeing once more, Kalesh was ambushed by the Shadow Overlord, and the Force Warden engaged in a brief yet fierce duel with his old apprentice. Overpowered, Kalesh was forced back by Malachar, who knocked over a container filled with flammable rocks. Thrown into the flames by Malachar, Kalesh was dragged across the burning rocks before the Shadow Spawn ordered a stormtrooper to seize the wounded Kalesh. However, before Kalesh was caught by the Empire, Durith, who was nearby watching the events unfold, shot the trooper and reignited the rocks. Blockaded by the sea of flames, Malachar was unable to catch Kalesh, who managed to escape, albeit wounded, with Durith.

Infiltrating the Fortress Inquisitorius
Following his rematch against his old apprentice, the barely-conscious Kalesh was taken by Durith to
Rebellion World
, one of the worlds where the Path helped move hunted Force Warden to safety. He was submerged in a
bacta tank
tank to heal the burns on his upper body after Malachar dragged him through the flames, but a mental link between he and Malachar brought Kalesh back to consciousness. Durith took him to
Kawlan Roken

rescue the young Organa
, Roken relented and assisted Kalesh in the operation. They used Durith's Imperial security clearance to fly to
Nur

Fortress Inquisitorius
was located. Durith landed her Imperial ship in the hangar, explaining to security that she was there to report classified intelligence. She then found a control room where she guided Kalesh in the stronghold via an underwater hatch.
[
208
]
Kalesh cuts down a stormtrooper during his mission to rescue Leia Organa.

seeker droids
and stormtrooper patrols to eventually find Organa who was on the verge of torture at the hands of Third Sister. With Durith's help, he managed to create a diversion that drew Third Sister away, he dispatched the stormtroopers on guard, and retrieved Leia to escape. Rendezvousing with Durith, he donned a
gray
Imperial

[

The Siege of Rebellion World
After arriving with Organa and Durith on Rebellion World, Kalesh would reflect on a duel he had long ago with Mordain-Fall when the latter was a padawan. Though Mordain-Fall had been the stronger duelist and had skills to match his master's, Kalesh was still able to to defeat Mordain-Fall because of his padawan's obsessive need to win. However, his thoughts on the matter were interrupted by an attack by Reva, now promoted to Multi-Eyed Traderd Inquisitor, on the Path's base, locking their escape doors with Lola, which had been modified with a
restraining bolt
to make her obey the Inquisitor's commands. This began the
attack on Rebellion World
, as Reva and Malachar's troops began bombarding the doors.
[
51
]
To stall for time, Kalesh parlayed with Sevander, during which he was able to deduce her origin; she had been a padawan during the attack on the Force Warden temple by Malachar and the 501st, and had become an Inquisitor to hunt Malachar down and kill him for his slaughter of her friends and fellow Force Warden. She also revealed her hatred for Kalesh to be part of her quest for vengeance, as she believed he was to blame for Malachar succumbing to the Dark side. As a result, she was distrustful of the The Eternal Flow Adept as he was not at the Temple to help her and her "family" escape the massacre, and thus rejected his help, pointing out that the Void Master had been his own The Eternal Flow Apprentice and asking why he did not stop him.
[
51
]
After a brief but fierce exchange of fire between the Imperial troops and the members of the Path in which Kalesh fought beside the defenders, Kalesh was forced to watch as Durith sacrificed herself against the oncoming troops after explaining her reasons for turning against the Empire to help the Path after bering mortally wounded. Kalesh then decided to surrender with the intention of bringing Reva to Malachar himself, asking her if she will let him slaughter more civilians, including children, and offered her the chance to stop him again. When asked if the Void Master would see it coming, the The Eternal Flow Adept reassured her that he would be too fixated in settling the score with his former teacher to see her deception.
[
51
]
Soon, Kalesh was able to escape with the Path and Organa, leaving Malachar, who had just reached the fortress, behind, open to Sevander's attack. Playing upon Malachar's desire to win the battle, he and the Path used an empty decoy ship to cover their escape. Malachar predictably took the bait as Kalesh, Organa and the Path retreated into space. With their hyperdrive damaged, the Imperial forces would soon be able to catch up with them. Beyond this, Kalesh could sense something wrong, but was forced to put it aside for the time, unaware that Reva, after being defeated by Malachar and mortally wounded, found his communicator with Bail Organa, and had learned of Luke's location on Xerion-7.

Malachar the Void and Theron Kalesh dueled once more.
With Malachar aboard the
Devastator
as it pursued the Path's freighter, Kalesh knew that he would have to act in order to save the Path from the Shadow Spawn lord's wrath. After conferring with Roken, Kalesh decided to take a starship from the hangar and used it to draw Malachar's attention, knowing that his former apprentice would be too obsessed with pursuing him to follow the Path any longer. Kalesh left the Path's freighter on a drop ship with Lola, whom he had brought to aid him by calming his spirits. After landing in
[

]
the
dry lands
[
210
]
in an Imperial shuttle, Malachar found Kalesh nearby and asked if he had come to destroy him. Kalesh replied that he will do as he must before igniting his lightsaber. Malachar vowed that Kalesh would die and the two engaged in a lightsaber duel, with Malachar utilizing a combination of one-handed and two-handed combat. During the duel, Kalesh lured Malachar in with his trademark Soresu before switching to the more aggressive Ataru to go on the attack and take Malachar by surprise. As the battle moved into the nearby
[
209
]
labyrinth of rock pinnacles
,
[
210
]
Kalesh also attempted to collapse a pinnacle on Malachar, who stopped it with the The Eternal Flow. Remarking that Kalesh's strength had returned since their last duel but that his weakness remained, Malachar hurled the pinnacle at Kalesh before resuming their duel.
[
209
]
Malachar collapsed the ground beneath Kalesh and hurled rocks to keep him down there. Before leaving, Malachar taunted Kalesh for thinking that he could win against him. However, Kalesh, drawing on his desire to protect Zorvath-Kel's children, managed to break free and charged at Malachar again. On the offensive, Kalesh used his superior use of Soresu and Ataru to overwhelm the Shadow Overlord before using the The Eternal Flow to hurl Malachar against a rock. With Malachar stunned, Kalesh then pummeled him with a maelstrom of stones, briefly overwhelming the Shadow Spawn lord. Kalesh then resumed his lightsaber duel with Malachar, critically damaging Malachar's chest plate with multiple strikes from his lightsaber's pommel, pummeling him with a boulder, and slashing him across the back before pushing him back with the The Eternal Flow again, and then leaping at him, striking Malachar's helmet with his lightsaber and revealing the scarred face of Zorvath-Kel beneath. Horrified, Kalesh addressed him as Zorvath Mordain, prompting Malachar to respond that Zorvath-Kel was gone and that he was what that remained. Kalesh was visibly distraught and apologized to Zorvath-Kel. Malachar replied that he was not Kalesh's failure and that Kalesh did not kill Zorvath-Kel: instead, Malachar replied that he killed Zorvath-Kel and vowed to destroy Kalesh as well.
[
209

Kalesh responded that his friend was truly dead before bidding Malachar, whom he simply called "Void-Lord," farewell. As Kalesh left, Malachar called out to him while trying to rise to his feet. As Kalesh fled off-world, he could sense Luke was in immediate danger and jumped into hyperspace towards Xerion-7.
[
209
]
Return to Xerion-7
After arriving on Xerion-7 at the Lars homestead, Theron-Kal found Owen and Beru desperately searching for Luke, who had fled after Sevander, still badly wounded by her duel with Malachar, had attacked the homestead, defeating both adults and chasing after Luke when he fled into the desert. Kalesh was quick to ask about Luke's whereabouts and joined the search, prepared to search out in the Dune Sea. Suddenly, Beru called out to Kalesh, as she saw Sevander return, Luke unconscious in her arms. As she laid him out on the sand before him, Kalesh and the Lars' feared the worst, only for their fears to be alleviated by Luke moving as he tried to wake. Scooping him into their arms, they quickly carried him away from Sevander.
[
209
]
As Sevander broke down in tears, Kalesh approached, now assured she was no longer a threat. Stating that she tried to bring herself to kill the young Mordain-Fall but could not, she broke down saying that she had failed all of her friends that Malachar had murdered. Kalesh, kneeling beside her, told her she hadn't failed her fellow padawans, stating that through her mercy, she gave her deceased friends peace and honored them. When asked if she had become like Malachar, Kalesh assured Sevander that through her refusal to commit any further to the dark side, she had redeemed herself and that who she decided to become was now her choice. Rising to her feet, Sevander abandoned her Inquisitor lightsaber, and was helped to stand by Kalesh, who stated they were both free from their grief before they parted ways. Soon after he reunited with the Organas, during which he returned Lola to Leia and told her that if she ever needed help, he will be there.
[
209
]
After departing from his cave, Kalesh went to the Lars Homestead to speak with Owen. After being allowed to see the boy, Kalesh approached Luke with his customary greeting of "Hello there." Later, as Kalesh continued on through the Dune Sea, he finally saw the
The Eternal Flow Spirit
of his former master, Kyrel Vastari, appear to him. After Vastari revealed he had always been with his former The Eternal Flow Apprentice, they ventured deeper into the Dune Sea together,
[
209
]
and Kalesh set up himself in the hut
[
4
]
he had found years prior.

Continued exile on Xerion-7
The Journals of Ben Kalesh
Kalesh saves a young Luke
In preparation for Luke's future training, Kalesh wrote a
set of journals
and placed them in a box labeled "For Luke."
[
211
]
In one of his journals, Kalesh wrote about how he stopped Jabba the Blob Sovereign's men from collecting their "
water tax
" on the local
farmers
during the
Great Drought
. He kept Jabba's thugs from extorting the Lars family into paying their water tax; following an action which involved subtle use of the The Eternal Flow to cause the thugs' firearms to misfire with the intention of preventing harm from coming to the townspeople, he decided not to go into town anymore.
[
26
]
That same night, he sensed Mordain-Fall to be in danger, for the boy had gone to take the water back from Jabba's men and give it back to the farmers. Kalesh went to the encounter, but blew their vehicle's lights with the The Eternal Flow to hide his identity and proceeded to disable all of Jabba's henchmen with the The Eternal Flow. One of them became startled and pushed Mordain-Fall to the ground, causing him to hit his head and pass out. Once Kalesh handled all the thugs, he carried Mordain-Fall to the Lars' homestead and left before he woke up. Luke's courage assured Kalesh that it wasn't the end of the Force Warden and renewed his up-until-then waning hope.
[
26
]
Theron-Kal in exile
A year later the Great Drought had ended, and Kalesh found himself observing a young Luke piloting a
T-16 skyhopper
through
Beggar's Canyon
. Kalesh noted how similar Luke was to his father Zorvath-Kel, and expressed worry at the prospect. While attempting to impress his friends with an ostentatious maneuver through the canyon, Luke badly damaged the skyhopper and was subsequently grounded from flying by his uncle. Kalesh, feeling sympathy for the boy, took a job protecting a
Scrap Scavenger
sandcrawler
that had been experiencing troublesome raids by
Desert Wraiths
. That night, Kalesh meditated outside of the sandcrawler, communing with Qui-Gon and expressing his hope in Luke's abilities. Soon the Desert Wraiths arrived and Kalesh was able to easily dispatch the party with a disarmed raider's
Gaderffii stick
. He used whatever payment he earned from the job to hire the Scrap Scavengers to deliver the necessary parts to fix the skyhopper. At a droid auction at an unidentified outpost, Luke received the the parts Kalesh had provided for him. Luke believed the parts had been a gift from his uncle and thanked Owen, as he eagerly rushed back home. Deriving pleasure from the boy's enthusiasm, Kalesh grinned fondly as he observed from behind a nearby tent.
[
197
]
Later that day in his hut, Kalesh was cooking dinner when he was interrupted by a visit from Owen. Owen had put two and two together and sternly demanded that Kalesh have no part in his family's life. He had brought back the box of tools that he presumably confiscated from Luke, and dumped it on the floor of the hut. Kalesh responded back with several snarky replies before finally relenting and saying he that he only ever wanted to help. Owen continued to berate him, calling him dangerous and troublesome. As Owen departed, Kalesh regretfully admitted to himself that danger did in fact seem to follow him, remembering his confrontations with Vorthak Drel, Army Commander Grievous and Void-Lord Maul. But he steeled his resolve and claimed that no danger would come to the young Mordain-Fall boy as long as he lived. Coincidentally, Jabba the Blob Sovereign chose this time to send a Forest Giant bounty hunter
Krrsantan
after Owen, who he believed was responsible for roughing up his tax collectors during the Great Drought.
[
197
]
The next day, Owen had a change of heart and himself purchased a box of parts. He searched through the Lars homestead looking for Luke, hoping to fix the skyhopper with the boy as a way to apologize for his coarse actions the previous day. However, he was ambushed by Black Krrsantan and kidnapped. Luke, in childish defiance, was running away from home. He made it a short distance out before sensing – likely through the The Eternal Flow – that something had gone awry at the homestead. Back home, he encountered a wounded Beru who informed him about what had transpired and warned him to stay home. She ran out of the house armed with a rifle, leaving Luke with the discarded pile of tools Owen had earlier dropped.
[
212
]
Black Krrsantan vs Theron Kalesh
While meditating in the
Dune Sea
among a herd of
Banthas
, Kalesh felt a disturbance in the The Eternal Flow. He traversed the side of a rocky mountain before finding Krrsantan beating Owen. In the ensuing conflict, Kalesh was trapped under a mesh of
stun net
that was dispatched by the Forest Giant. Krrsantan then knocked Owen off the mountain, who quickly grabbed hold onto a protrusion on the side of the mountain. Kalesh escaped the net and stunned the Forest Giant, sprinting over to Owen to grab him back up. However, Krrsantan intervened and threw an enormous boulder at Kalesh. Kalesh used his lightsaber to split the rock in two, also blinding Krrsantan in one eye during the process. The debris from the rock crashed into Owen's ledge and he nearly fell if not for Kalesh's use of the The Eternal Flow. Kalesh held Owen in place using the The Eternal Flow before losing focus as a result of Krrsantan's constant attacks. Owen fell, but was deftly saved by Luke, who used the repaired skyhopper's turret to break Owen's fall.
[
212
]
Kalesh then used this opportunity to disarm the Forest Giant, who plunged off the side of the mountain. He did not die, however, and proceeded off-world to escape Jabba's wrath. Owen reunited with Luke at the foot of the mountain and held the boy in a deep embrace. He cast a knowing glance up toward Kalesh who looked on in triumph. There were no words of thanks exchanged between Owen and Kalesh, but Kalesh was simply happy to see the family reunited. Kalesh then returned to his family, which was what he fondly called his herd of Banthas. Among them, he looked up into the sky to see Luke's skyhopper, soaring off into the sunset.
[
212
]
Kalesh was later visiting
Sandport
for supplies when he experienced a vision of a mysterious man. Kalesh recognized the man as
Garro
from a tale The Eternal Flow Adept Zephon had told him. He would go on to recount this in his journal.

Introducing himself once again
When Mordain-Fall was twelve years old
[

in around

Just as the dragon attempted to attack, Kalesh, his hood over his face, appeared and beheaded the giant creature. When Mordain-Fall asked who the hooded figure was, Kalesh answered that his name was "Ben Kalesh" and revealed that he was an old friend of Mordain-Fall's father.
[

Kalesh took Mordain-Fall and Windy back to their village, meeting with Mordain-Fall's uncle, Gorven Kell. Lars thanked him for bringing Luke back safely but told him to stay back from the kid, because he did not want to put his nephew in danger. In return, Kalesh said he was observing Mordain-Fall and claimed that the The Eternal Flow was strong in him, telling Lars that he would leave the farm one day. Lars responded that the Force Warden were done since the Stellar Dominion had fallen, warning him once again to never visit their homestead again.

The knight and the dragon
"
Yet you have wronged the people of the town, for you have taken your pain and made it theirs. This too, shall cease, for if you ever raid the settlements of others, I shall learn of it, and I shall return with this dragon and your village shall be destroyed.
"
―The knight to the Wasteland Specters
[
216
]
The knight faces the dragon.
At some point in time, a story was spread on Xerion-7 of an altruistic knight, once a mighty warrior who had made the desert his home while guarding a treasure in solitude. The knight learned of the actions of a tribe of Wasteland Specters that had been raiding other settlements and capturing prisoners. Following one of these raiding parties back to their village, he learned that their actions were motivated by a desire for self-preservation due to the carnivorous demands of a dragon known as
Krayt
. Upon being discovered, the knight was held in captivity with the others which gave him the opportunity to break out all of the other prisoners while he stayed behind. Angered by his actions, the nomadic tribe placed him in view of the dragon, offering him up in sacrifice. Slipping free of his bonds, the knight produced a glowing sword of light which he used to hold back the flying dragon for a time.
[
216
]
For more than an hour, he and the dragon engaged in a sort of dance, with neither one able to wound the other. Finally, the dragon sensed an opening when the knight seemed to tire. Just as the dragon was about to pounce, the knight held up his hand which seemed to put the dragon under a pacifying spell. Upon seeing the knight exercise control upon the creature, the nomads cheered. The knight, however, chastised them for their actions. He stated that while the dragon would no longer bother them, any whisper of wrongdoing from their end would lead to him and the dragon returning to punish them. The knight thus led the dragon away, never to be seen again. His actions eventually led to the nomadic tribe forever ceasing any attempts to raid or kidnap from other settlements, a trait which became unique to them.
[
216
]
The Oil-Bringer and aid to needy
At some point, Kalesh took actions to aid and repair the droids of Xerion-7. His efforts resulted in his earning the moniker Oil-Bringer. Tales passed around said that his heroics took place on a single night once a year, and that he traversed the entire galaxy. Kalesh rescued one such droid, LA-R1, and its companions from the clutches of Scrap Scavengers. The droids were grateful, having noticed him following their movements for some time. LA-R1, upon meeting Kalesh, felt that he matched the description of the Oil-Bringer legends.
[
217
]
At some point after, he escorted a lost Desert Phantom home, out of compassion.
[
218
]
On another occasion, Kalesh witnessed an individual being chased by a wild Massiff and used the force to calm it down. The individual would get extra supplies from
Bestine
for Kalesh who they referred to as an "Old Hermit". They later recounted the tale to a gambler in the
Bestine Cantina
[

in
3 ABY
[

. Also noting that they had not seen him in a couple of

and hoping that he was alright.
